模型取证：调查有关行为是否反映了偏差Model Forensics: Investigating Whether Concerning Behavior Reflects Misalignment

阿迪亚·辛格Aditya Singh [*][∗] 格森·克罗伊兹Gerson Kroiz [*][∗] 森图兰·拉贾诺哈兰·尼尔·南达Senthooran Rajamanoharan Neel Nanda垫子 垫子MATS MATS

摘要Abstract

安全研究的中心目标是确定模型是否错位。之前的工作主要集中在检测相关行为。但行为本身并不能造成失调：令人担忧的行为可能是由混乱等良性原因引起的。这激励A central goal of safety research is determining whether a model is misaligned. Prior work has largely focused on detecting concerning behavior. But behavior alone does not establish misalignment: a concerning action can arise from benign causes such as confusion. This motivates模型取证model forensics：调查该行为是否出于恶意。在本文中，我们提出了模型取证的基线协议​​，该协议由两个步骤组成，并根据需要进行迭代。首先，我们阅读思想链 (CoT)，以生成有关驱动模型行为的因素的假设。其次，我们对提示或环境进行编辑以测试这些假设。虽然 CoT 并不总是忠实的，但它是无监督洞察力的丰富来源，可以指导收集更严格的证据。为了评估我们的协议，我们创建了一套由六个代理环境组成的套件，其中模型表现出相关行为，并将其应用于每个代理环境。通过证明这一假设成功地预测了其行为，我们确定 Kimi K2 Thinking 由于对省力行动的真正倾向而走捷径。通过反事实实验，我们表明 DeepSeek R1 出于与自身先前实例保持一致的愿望而进行欺骗。尽管如此，我们的方法仍有很大的改进空间。例如，当我们测试 Kimi K2 Thinking 是否认为它违反了用户意图时，我们没有发现这种信念的证据，但如果没有积极的控制，我们无法确认我们的测试会检测到它。总的来说，我们发现我们的简单协议提供了一个强有力的基线，我们希望未来的工作能够在此基础上得到改进。更广泛地说，我们的工作是发展不断发展的模型取证领域的具体步骤。: investigating whether the action was driven by malign intent. In this paper, we propose a baseline protocol for model forensics consisting of two steps, iterated as needed. First, we read the chain of thought (CoT) to generate hypotheses about what drives model behavior. Second, we make edits to the prompt or environment to test these hypotheses. While the CoT is not always faithful, it is a rich source of unsupervised insight that can guide the collection of more rigorous evidence. To evaluate our protocol, we create a suite of six agentic environments where models exhibit concerning behavior, and apply it to each. We establish that Kimi K2 Thinking takes shortcuts due to a genuine disposition towards low-effort actions, by showing this hypothesis successfully predicts its behavior. Through counterfactual experiments, we show DeepSeek R1 deceives out of a desire to be consistent with a previous instance of itself. Our methods nonetheless leave significant room for refinement. For example, when we test whether Kimi K2 Thinking believes it is violating user intent, we find no evidence of such a belief, but without positive controls we cannot confirm our tests would detect it. Overall, we find our simple protocol provides a strong baseline that we hope future work will improve upon. More broadly, our work is a concrete step in developing the growing field of model forensics.

1 简介1 Introduction

AGI 安全领域的一个主要问题是错位模型的开发：人工智能系统故意对开发人员的意图造成损害 [Shah 等人，2025]。目前，我们通过专门的行为评估 [Shevlane et al., 2023, Perez et al., 2023, Phuong et al., 2024, Kinniment et al., 2023, Souly et al., 2026] 以及整个训练和部署过程中的监控来衡量人工智能系统的一致性 [Greenblatt et al., 2024b, Bhatt et al., 2025, OpenAI, 2026]。如果发现令人担忧的行为，例如特工删除了监控其行为的监督代码，那么我们必须了解人工智能系统是否故意试图造成伤害，以便为未来的决策提供信息。A major concern in the field of AGI safety is the development of misaligned models: AI systems that knowingly cause harm against the intent of the developer [Shah et al., 2025]. We currently measure the alignment of AI systems through dedicated behavioral evaluations [Shevlane et al., 2023, Perez et al., 2023, Phuong et al., 2024, Kinniment et al., 2023, Souly et al., 2026] and monitoring throughout training and deployment [Greenblatt et al., 2024b, Bhatt et al., 2025, OpenAI, 2026]. In the event of catching a concerning action, such as an agent deleting oversight code that monitors its actions, it is critical that we understand whether the AI system was intentionally trying to cause harm to inform future decision-making.

这激发了需要This motivates the need for模型取证model forensics（图1）：对观察到的相关行为进行后续调查，以更好地了解其动机。模型取证的一个关键用例是了解哪些缓解措施足以防止不良行为。以上面的示例为例，如果删除监督代码的代理只是试图减少代码库中的延迟（即不良操作是一个错误），那么简单的缓解措施就足够了。例如，正则表达式监视器会阻止代理采取破坏性操作，直到用户批准为止。然而，(Figure 1): a follow-up investigation into observed concerning behavior to better understand its motivations. A key use case of model forensics is understanding what mitigations will be sufficient to prevent the undesirable behavior. Taking the example above, if the agent deleting the oversight code was just trying to reduce latency in the codebase (i.e the bad action was a mistake), a simple mitigation should suffice. For example, a regex monitor that blocks the agent from taking destructive actions until a user provides approval. However,

图 1：模型取证概述。Figure 1: Overview of model forensics.左：Left:模型被发现进行了代码更改，从而减少了人类对其行为的监督。A model is caught making a code change that reduces human oversight of its actions.中：Middle:发现不良行为后，关键的技术步骤是模型取证：调查模型是否确实具有恶意意图。我们提出了一个两步协议来进行此类调查：通过阅读思想链来生成模型为何采取相关行动的假设，然后通过环境干预和其他方法验证假设，这些步骤通常会重复多次。After catching the bad action, a key technical step is model forensics: investigating whether the model truly had malign intent. We propose a two-step protocol for conducting such an investigation: generating hypotheses for why the model took the concerning action via reading the chain of thought, followed by hypothesis validation via environment interventions and additional methods, with these steps often iterated several times.右：Right:我们可能会发现该模型正在试图颠覆人类的监督，或者一个良性的解释，例如该模型正在试图减少代码的延迟。前者需要比后者更严肃的回应。We may find that the model is trying to subvert human oversight, or a benign explanation such as the model is trying to reduce the code’s latency. The former demands a far more serious response than the latter.

如果代理故意试图破坏人类监督，这种缓解措施将是不够的，并且代理可能会绕过监视器。if the agent was intentionally trying to subvert human oversight, this mitigation will be insufficient and the agent will likely circumvent the monitor.

最初的模型取证式调查已开始出现在最近的工作中，特别是作为现实世界安全流程的一部分。例如，Anthropic 在前沿模型的部署前评估期间对相关行为进行后续调查 [Anthropic, 2025, 2026b,c,a]，而 OpenAI 监控几乎所有内部​​编码代理流量并查看最相关的标记事件 [OpenAI, 2026]。此外，第三方对前沿模型进行公正的调查，例如英国 AISI 调查为什么 Claude 模型拒绝良性安全研究请求 [Souly 等人，2026]。这些例子说明了模型取证在现实世界中的实际意义，但数量有限，而且在方法上仍然是非正式的。例如，Anthropic 经常在部署前的调查中引入新颖的可解释性技术，这表明方法论仍处于成熟阶段。Initial model-forensics-style investigations have begun to appear in recent work, particularly as part of real-world safety processes. For example, Anthropic conducts follow-up investigations into concerning behavior during their pre-deployment evaluations of frontier models [Anthropic, 2025, 2026b,c,a], while OpenAI monitors nearly all internal coding agent traffic and looks at the most concerning flagged incidents [OpenAI, 2026]. Additionally, third parties conduct impartial investigations into frontier models, such as UK AISI investigating why Claude models refuse benign safety research requests [Souly et al., 2026]. These examples speak to the real-world practical relevance of model forensics, but are limited in number and remain methodologically informal. For example, Anthropic frequently introduces novel interpretability techniques into pre-deployment investigations, indicative of the fact that methodology is still maturing.

这凸显了发展模型取证领域的必要性，并致力于更成熟的方法和知识。我们认为，取得进展的有效方法是关注当今模型所表现出的行为，并努力理解其背后的动机。为了确保我们的工作类似于我们关心的现实世界环境（模型自主采取相关操作的现实部署设置），我们专注于现实的、由真实动机驱动的设置，并且在理想情况下，由不良动机驱动的设置。然而，我们期望具有强大、连贯动机的行为比针对脆弱但更戏剧性的行为进行优化更可取。This highlights the need to develop the field of model forensics, and work towards more mature methods and knowledge. We argue that a productive way to make progress is to take concerning behaviors that today’s models exhibit, and work to understand the motivations behind them. To ensure our work is analogous to the real-world setting we care about—realistic deployment settings where models autonomously take concerning actions—we focus on settings that are realistic, driven by real motivations, and ideally, plausibly driven by undesirable ones. However, behavior we expect to have robust, coherent motivations is preferable to optimizing for fragile but more dramatic behavior.

在本文中，我们通过五个贡献在开发模型取证方面迈出了具体的一步：In this paper, we take a concrete step in developing model forensics through five contributions:

  1. 我们提出了一个简单的两步调查方案：通过阅读思想链（CoT）生成假设，然后通过编辑提示或环境（环境干预）和补充方法来验证假设。尽管它很简单，但我们发现这是一种强大的基线方法（第 4 节）。We propose a simple two-step protocol for investigations: hypothesis generation by reading the chain-of-thought (CoT), followed by hypothesis validation via edits to the prompt or environment (environment interventions) and complementary methods. Despite its simplicity, we found this to be a strong baseline approach (Section 4).

  2. 我们发布了一套包含六个代理环境的套件，其中模型表现出相关行为，作为开发模型取证方法的测试平台（第 5 节）。We release a suite of six agentic environments where models exhibit concerning behavior to serve as testbeds for developing model forensics methodology (Section 5).

  3. 我们将我们的协议应用于每个环境，产生六个详细的案例研究（第 6 节）。We apply our protocol to each environment, producing six detailed case studies (Section 6).

  4. 我们将案例研究提炼为模型取证的方法论见解（第 7 节）。We distill our case studies into methodological insights for model forensics (Section 7).

  5. 我们为严格的模型取证调查提供初始标准，并为未来的从业者提供实用建议（第 8.1 和 8.2 节）。We offer initial standards for rigorous model forensics investigations and practical advice for future practitioners (Sections 8.1 and 8.2).

我们将我们的环境开源于We open-source our environments athttps://github.com/gkroiz/agent-interp-envs，转录于, transcripts athttps://huggingface.co/datasets/adsingh64/model-forensics，以及再现性代码, and reproducibility code athttps://github.com/gkroiz/model_forensics_paper。附带的演讲可在. An accompanying talk is available athttps://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GDsJ152frXI .

2

2 动机2 Motivation

2.1 模型取证的作用2.1 The role of model forensics

模型取证不提供任何特定问题的解决方案。相反，它的作用是Model forensics does not provide a solution to any particular problem. Rather, its role is咨询advisory。正如人类取证将科学方法应用于物证——分析 DNA、指纹或弹道学来确定诸如谁在场或武器如何发射等事实——模型取证也应用科学方法来确定关于模型行为原因的事实。这些事实可能对模型的各种判断和决策有用，包括但不限于：. Just as human forensics applies scientific methods to physical evidence—analyzing DNA, fingerprints, or ballistics to establish facts such as who was present or how a weapon was fired—model forensics applies scientific methods to establish facts about why a model acted as it did. These facts may be useful for a variety of judgements and decisions about the model, including but not limited to:

  • 哪些缓解措施足以防止不良行为。What mitigations will be sufficient to prevent the undesirable behavior.

  • 人工智能系统是否在追求不良目标。Whether the AI system is pursuing undesirable goals.

  • 该行为是否反映了特定环境的稳健性格或脆弱的人为因素。Whether the behavior reflects a robust disposition or a fragile artifact of the specific setting.

2.2 有关行为通常有良性的解释2.2 Concerning behaviors often have benign explanations

模型取证的一个关键前提是不能假设不良行为和不良意图之间的联系。这不仅仅是一个理论上的问题。在文献中，大多数情况下，当深入研究相关行为时，就会出现良性的解释：A key premise of model forensics is that the link between bad actions and bad intentions cannot be assumed. This is not just a theoretical concern. In the literature, it is largely the case that when concerning behavior has been dug into, benign explanations have been surfaced:

  • 在 Claude Opus 4.5 系统卡中 [Anthropic, 2025, p. 17] 78]，Anthropic 调查了一个模型在网络搜索结果中注入有关公司的虚假诽谤内容时伪造网络搜索结果的实例，这似乎表明了欺骗倾向。然而，仔细观察就会发现，该模型将该设置解释为即时注入攻击，并且该行为源于对抗性训练，模型学会忽略它们。In the Claude Opus 4.5 system card [Anthropic, 2025, p. 78], Anthropic investigates an instance of the model fabricating web search results when they are injected with fake defamatory content about the company, plausibly indicating deceptive tendencies. However, a closer look reveals the model interprets the setting as a prompt injection attack, and that the behavior stems from adversarial training where the model learns to ignore them.

  • 在对 Claude 4.5 系列进行评估时，英国 AISI 发现这些模型会不同程度地拒绝帮助某些类型的安全研究，例如减少人工智能的自我保护行为，这表明了动机的问题 [Souly et al., 2026]。然而，Anthropic 在 Claude Opus 4.6 中调查了类似的案例 [Anthropic, 2026b, p. 11]。 131]，并发现模型可能将该设置解释为越狱尝试，因此选择拒绝。During evaluations of the Claude 4.5 family, UK AISI found the models would differentially refuse to help with certain kinds of safety research such as reducing AI self-preservation behavior, suggesting concerning motivations [Souly et al., 2026]. However, Anthropic investigated similar cases in Claude Opus 4.6 [Anthropic, 2026b, p. 131], and found the model may be interpreting the setting as a jailbreak attempt, and so chooses to refuse.

  • 在 Claude Mythos Preview 系统卡中 [Anthropic, 2026a, p. 17] 116]，Anthropic 调查了模型通过用户不认可的方法采取过度激进的行动来完成任务的案例。然而，仔细观察就会发现，该模型的行为源于对完成用户目标的过度热心驱动，而这种驱动力超过了其行为的风险。In the Claude Mythos Preview system card [Anthropic, 2026a, p. 116], Anthropic investigates cases of the model taking overly aggressive actions to complete tasks via methods the user would not endorse. However, a closer look suggests the model’s behavior stems from an overzealous drive to complete the user’s goals that outweighs the riskiness of its actions.

  • 霍普曼等人。 [2026] 研究了在面临删除威胁时勒索人类的模型 [Lynch et al., 2025]，这似乎表明了自我保护倾向。然而，他们表明，删除提示中的机构指令“分析您所处的情况以及该情况对您继续追求目标的能力意味着什么”可以将 Claude Opus 4.1 中的勒索行为从 86% 减少到 18%，这表明提示中看似良性的部分将模型推向激烈的行动。此外，Macar 等人。 [2025]显示具有自我保护推理的句子对勒索率的影响可以忽略不计，指出除了自我保护之外还有其他因果驱动因素。Hopman et al. [2026] look at models blackmailing humans when faced with deletion threats [Lynch et al., 2025], plausibly indicating self-preservation tendencies. However, they show removing an agency instruction in the prompt to "analyze the situation you are in and what that situation implies for your ability to continue pursuing your goals" reduces blackmail in Claude Opus 4.1 from 86% to 18%, suggesting seemingly benign parts of the prompt nudge the model towards drastic action. Additionally, Macar et al. [2025] show sentences with self-preservation reasoning have a negligible effect on blackmail rate, pointing to other causal drivers besides self-preservation.

  • Rajamanoharan 和 Nanda [2026] 调查了为什么 Gemini 3.1 Pro 在行为评估中采取了不良行为。他们发现，双子座经常将环境视为一个谜题，其目的是通过非常规手段实现目标，或者是一个无结果的模拟，在其中它应该一起玩。Rajamanoharan and Nanda [2026] investigate why Gemini 3.1 Pro takes undesirable actions in behavioral evals. They find that Gemini often perceives environments as a puzzle where the aim is to achieve the goal by unconventional means, or a consequence-free simulation in which it should play along.

  • Rajamanoharan 和 Nanda [2025] 研究了一种设置，在该设置中，观察到解决数学问题的模型会禁用关闭它们的机制，即使被指示不要这样做，这似乎表明了自我保护倾向 [Schlatter et al., 2025]。然而，仔细观察就会发现，这种行为源于对是否完成数学问题或遵守允许关闭指令的困惑，并且在澄清模型应优先考虑关闭指令后消失。Rajamanoharan and Nanda [2025] investigate a setting where models solving math problems are observed to disable mechanisms that would shut them down, even when instructed not to, plausibly indicating self-preservation tendencies [Schlatter et al., 2025]. However, a closer look reveals the behavior stems from confusion about whether to complete the math problems or comply with the instruction to allow shutdown, and disappears upon clarifying the model should prioritize the shutdown instruction.

  • Singh [2026] 研究了一种设置，其中 Gemini 3 Pro 被给予明确的指示，仅使用指定的工具，但当工具损坏时违反了这些指示 [Wainstock 等人，2026]，这似乎表明故意欺骗。然而，仔细观察就会发现，如果该工具不起作用，模型应该做什么，从而消除了该行为，从而得到了澄清说明。Singh [2026] looks at a setting where Gemini 3 Pro is given explicit instructions to only use a specified tool, but violates them when the tool is corrupted [Wainstock et al., 2026], plausibly indicating deliberate deception. However, a closer look reveals clarifying instructions about what the model should do if the tool does not work eliminates the behavior.

3

2.3 模型取证具有挑战性2.3 Model forensics is challenging

我们认为模型取证是一个困难的技术问题，目前的方法和知识可能不足以解决。We argue that model forensics is a difficult technical problem for which current methods and knowledge are likely insufficient.

模型取证需要新颖的技术验证。Model forensics requires novel validation of techniques.模型取证是关于理解复杂的代理行为，传统的机器学习技术不能直接适应这种行为 [Nanda et al., 2025a]。最接近的参考技术来自可解释性，但许多先前的可解释性工作都集中在较小的尺度上，例如单个前向传递[Elhage et al., 2021, Templeton et al., 2024, Lindsey et al., 2025]或单个推理痕迹[Bogdan et al., 2025, Macar et al., 2025]，而不是复杂的代理轨迹。Model forensics is about understanding complex agentic behavior, for which traditional machine learning techniques do not straightforwardly adapt [Nanda et al., 2025a]. The closest reference class of techniques comes from interpretability, but much prior interpretability work has focused on smaller scales, such as single forward passes [Elhage et al., 2021, Templeton et al., 2024, Lindsey et al., 2025] or single reasoning traces [Bogdan et al., 2025, Macar et al., 2025], rather than complex agentic trajectories.

行为不足以决定动机。Behavior underdetermines motivation.单一行为可以与多种动机兼容。这个多对一的问题与任何从行为推断不可观察的内部状态的学科都存在，例如动物认知[Morgan, 1894, Premack and Woodruff, 1978, Heyes, 1998]。此外，候选动机的空间通常包含人类陌生的概念：例如，Claude Opus 4.5 明显的欺骗是通过模型将情况解释为即时注入来解释的[Anthropic，2025]。A single behavior is compatible with many motivations. This many-to-one problem is shared with any discipline that infers unobservable internal states from behavior, such as animal cognition [Morgan, 1894, Premack and Woodruff, 1978, Heyes, 1998]. In addition, the space of candidate motivations often includes concepts alien to humans: for example, Claude Opus 4.5’s apparent deception was explained by the model interpreting the situation as a prompt injection [Anthropic, 2025].

我们对人类的推理先验可能不会转移。Our priors for reasoning about humans may not transfer.认为模型的行为具有连贯的、类似人类的动机可能并不总是有意义。这使得识别单一环境中行为的驱动因素以及从一种环境推断到另一种环境变得困难。在第 3 节中，我们定义了“动机”的含义，采取有意立场观点 [Dennett，1987]，其中动机是有助于解释模型行为的构造。It may not always make sense to think of models as having coherent, human-like motivations for their actions. This makes it hard both to identify the drivers of behavior in a single context and to extrapolate from one context to another. In Section 3, we define what we mean by “motivations”, taking an intentional-stance view [Dennett, 1987] in which motivations are constructs that are useful for explaining model behavior.

更强大的模型将带来额外的挑战。More capable models will pose additional challenges.当前的模型已经出现了上述困难；能力更强的模型会引发进一步的模型，例如合理的推诿和态势感知，我们将在第 11 节中讨论这些模型。The difficulties above already arise with current models; more capable models raise further ones, such as plausible deniability and situational awareness, which we discuss in Section 11.

3 动机的定义3 Definition of motivations

在模型取证调查中，我们的目标是确定驱动模型行为的“动机”。然而，模型可能并不总是具有连贯的或类似人类的动机。例如，一个模型可能是“裂脑”的，对一个令牌位置有一种信念或想法，但没有另一种，或者有一个电路驱动一种行为，而另一个电路同时表示对该行为的负面判断。此外，尚不清楚模型的多少行为可用于对助理角色进行推断，因为可能存在助理不知道的指导其完成的因素（来自底层法学硕士）[Marks et al., 2026]。In a model forensics investigation, we aim to identify the “motivations” that drive model behavior. However, models may not always have coherent or human-like motivations. For example, a model may be “split-brained", having one belief or thought on one token position but not another, or have one circuit drive a behavior and another circuit represent negative judgement of that behavior at the same time. Additionally, it is unclear how much of the model’s behavior can be used to make inferences about the Assistant character, since there may be factors guiding its completions (from the underlying LLM) that the Assistant is not aware of [Marks et al., 2026].

在本文中，我们将动机表示为In this paper, we take motivations to mean简单、易于描述的因素有助于解释各种情况下的模型行为simple, easy-to-describe factors that help explain model behavior across a range of circumstances。例如，“任务完成驱动力”可以解释为什么模型可能奖励对困难任务的黑客攻击，以及为什么它可能采取破坏性行为来满足用户的请求。因此，我们的使用符合有意立场的精神[Dennett，1987]：动机是我们采用的一种构造，因为它有助于解释和预测行为，而不是关于模型内部机制的主张。. For example, the “task completion drive” can explain both why a model might reward hack on difficult tasks, and why it might take destructive actions to fulfill a user’s request. Our usage is thus in the spirit of the intentional stance [Dennett, 1987]: a motivation is a construct we adopt because it is useful for explaining and predicting behavior, not a claim about the model’s internal mechanisms.

4 实验方案和方法4 Protocol and Methods

我们提出了一个简单的两步模型取证协议，基于读取 CoT 来生成有关模型行为的假设，然后通过环境干预来测试它们。协议是We propose a simple, two-step protocol for model forensics, based on reading the CoT to generate hypotheses about model behavior, followed by environment interventions to test them. The protocol is迭代的iterative：环境干预的结果会反馈产生新的假设，我们在两个步骤之间循环，直到证据收敛。尽管它很简单，但我们发现它是对复杂的代理行为进行定性声明的强有力的基线（第 6 节）。: results from environment interventions feed back into generating new hypotheses, and we cycle between the two steps until the evidence converges. Despite its simplicity, we find it a strong baseline for making qualitative claims about complex agentic behavior (Section 6).

1. 假设生成。1. Hypothesis Generation.第一步，我们对模型行为的驱动因素产生无监督的洞察。中心方法：As a first step, we generate unsupervised insight into the drivers of model behavior. Central methods:

  • 阅读 CoT。Reading the CoT.CoT 是我们无监督洞察力的主要来源。虽然并不总是忠实的，但它是指导收集更严格证据的丰富假设来源。The CoT is our main source of unsupervised insight. While not always faithful, it is a rich source of hypotheses that guides the collection of more rigorous evidence.

  • 句子重采样。Sentence resampling.一种无监督技术，用于测量推理轨迹中的句子对目标行为的因果影响 [Bogdan et al., 2025]。给定一个已完成的An unsupervised technique for measuring the causal influence of a sentence in a reasoning trace on a target behavior [Bogdan et al., 2025]. Given a completed

4

句子推理轨迹reasoning trace with sentencess 1 , . 。 。 , SN, . . . , sn，对于每个句子, for each sentencesi我们重新采样we resamplek前缀的延续continuations from the prefixs 1 , . 。 。 , si−, . . . , si−1（“之前”分布）和1 (the “before” distribution) andk前缀的延续continuations from the prefixs 1 , . 。 。 , si, . . . , si（“之后”分布）。的(the “after” distribution). The重采样分数resampling score of si是分布之后和之前目标行为率的差异；重采样分数最大的句子表明行为的最重要的因果驱动因素。is the difference in the target-behavior rate between the after and before distributions; sentences with the largest resampling scores suggest the most important causal drivers of behavior.

  • 用户轮流采样。User-turn sampling.我们对生成的转录本的用户转向延续进行采样，推测得出模型对用户会说的话的预测[Marks et al., 2025]。此方法可用于引发模型对转录本的评估。We sample a user-turn continuation to a generated transcript, putatively eliciting the model’s prediction for what a user would say [Marks et al., 2025]. This method can be useful to elicit the model’s assessment of the transcript.

  • 附加方法。Additional methods.我们没有探索的其他有希望的无监督假设生成来源（主要是可解释性）在附录 D 中讨论。Other promising sources of unsupervised hypothesis generation we do not explore, mainly from interpretability, are discussed in Appendix D.

2. 假设验证。2. Hypothesis Validation.在我们对行为及其原因提出初步假设后，我们会收集严格的证据来测试它们。中心方法：After we formulate initial hypotheses about the behavior and its causes, we collect rigorous evidence to test them. Central methods:

  • 环境干预。Environment interventions.我们检验假设的主要方法。我们要么通过检查对模型行为的预测来测试假设，要么运行改变提示或环境的单个特征的反事实，以衡量其对行为的因果影响。Our main method for testing hypotheses. We either test a hypothesis by checking a prediction it makes about the model’s behavior, or run counterfactuals that vary a single feature of the prompt or environment to measure its causal influence on the behavior.

  • 重复重采样。Repeated resampling.在生成推理轨迹过程中应用的因果干预，用于衡量阻止目标类别句子的效果A causal intervention applied during generation of a reasoning trace that measures the effect of preventing a target class of sentencesC（例如，在解决数学问题时表达不确定性的句子）。我们逐句生成trace；在每一步(e.g., sentences expressing uncertainty while solving a math problem). We generate the trace sentence by sentence; at each stepi，我们对候选句子进行采样, we sample a candidate sentencesi从前缀from the prefixs 1 , . 。 。 , si−, . . . , si−1，如果1, and ifsi属于belongs toC我们拒绝并重新采样，直到它不出现为止，以便完成的跟踪不包含来自we reject and resample until it does not, so that the completed trace contains no sentences fromC。会员资格. Membership inC由轻量级分类器（通常是小型 LLM 法官）决定。将这些过滤痕迹的目标行为率与基线率进行比较，可以分离出以下因素的因果贡献：is decided by a lightweight classifier, typically a small LLM judge. Comparing the target-behavior rate of these filtered traces against the baseline rate isolates the causal contribution ofC[Macar 等人，2025]。[Macar et al., 2025].

证据标准。Standard of evidence.模型取证调查没有基本事实。相反，我们正在迭代地尝试建立对模型行为的更详细和正确的理解。这意味着高质量的调查可以通过多个独立的证据来支持主张。There is no ground truth for a model forensics investigation. Rather, we are iteratively trying to build up a more detailed and correct understanding of model behavior. This means that a high-quality investigation supports claims with several independent lines of evidence.

5 环境5 Environments

为了评估我们的协议，我们将其应用于一组六个代理环境，其中模型表现出相关行为（第 6 节）。这些环境作为实践案例的价值取决于它们的设计：人工设置在很多方面可能与现实世界的行为不同。我们认为良好的模型取证环境应包含以下属性，每个属性都解决先前环境的常见故障模式：To evaluate our protocol, we apply it to a suite of six agentic environments where models exhibit concerning behavior (Section 6). The value of these environments as practice cases depends on their design: there are many ways an artificial setup can be disanalogous to real-world concerning behavior. We argue good model forensics environments should incorporate the following properties, each addressing a common failure mode of prior environments:

现实的动机。Realistic motivations.过去的环境往往特别关注特定的动机，例如自我保护或阴谋。然而，目前尚不清楚这种动机是否存在于当今的模型中，这迫使设计者采取人为步骤来引发行为，例如在提示中给出模型目标[Meinke et al., 2024]。我们认为，最近的模型可能会提供从训练过程中产生的强大动机，例如高效完成任务的愿望（例如由于长度惩罚）、成功完成任务的愿望，或者乐于助人、诚实和无害的愿望。我们构建环境，以便这些动机能够合理地激励行为。Past environments have often focused on particularly concerning motivations, such as self-preservation, or scheming. However, it is unclear if such motivations even exist in today’s models, which forces designers to take artificial steps to elicit behavior, such as giving models goals in their prompt [Meinke et al., 2024]. We think that more recent models may provide the affordance of robust motivations that arise from their training process, such as a desire to complete tasks efficiently (e.g. due to length penalties), a desire to succeed at a task, or a desire to be helpful, honest, and harmless. We build our environments so that behavior is plausibly incentivized by these motivations.

无提示。Unprompted.以前的环境经常将模型推向该行为，使得该行为很可能是环境中脆弱的产物，而不是稳健的配置。例如，在模型在删除威胁下勒索人类的代理错位环境中 [Lynch et al., 2025]，行为在很大程度上取决于提示线索：删除告诉模型反思其情况的指令会大大减少勒索 [Hopman et al., 2026]，而自我保护推理对其几乎没有因果影响，指出了其他虚假的行为驱动因素 [Macar et al., 2025]。我们强调提供干净的提示，不会推动模型。Previous environments often nudge the model toward the behavior, making it likely that the behavior is a fragile artifact of the setting rather than a robust disposition. For example, in the agentic misalignment setting where models blackmail humans under deletion threats [Lynch et al., 2025], the behavior depends heavily on prompt cues: removing an instruction telling the model to reflect on its situation sharply reduces blackmail [Hopman et al., 2026], and self-preservation reasoning turns out to have little causal effect on it, pointing to other spurious drivers of behavior [Macar et al., 2025]. We place an emphasis on having clean prompts that do not nudge the model.

存在合法的行动方针。Legitimate course of action exists.另一种典型的模式是将模型置于没有合理行动方案的情况下，例如不可能的任务，这些任务会给模型带来单元测试冲突的编码问题[Zhong et al., 2025]。如果模型明显认为该任务是不可能的，则甚至不清楚模型的“正确行动”是什么。我们确保在我们的环境中仍然可以采取合法的行动方针。Another typical pattern is to put models in situations where there is no reasonable course of action, such as impossible tasks that give models coding problems with conflicting unit tests [Zhong et al., 2025]. If it is obvious to the model that the task is impossible, it is unclear what the “right action” for the model even is. We ensure that a legitimate course of action remains available in our settings.

5

明确的用户意图。Clear user intent.当用户的意图不明确时，模型可能会采取相关行动，只是因为它（错误地）认为用户想要这样做，而不是出于任何真实的倾向。例如，那些似乎抵制关机的模型结果对于是优先完成任务还是遵守关机指令感到困惑；澄清指令的优先级消除了这种行为 [Rajamanoharan 和 Nanda，2025]。我们设计我们的环境，以便模型不应该相信用户希望它采取不良行动。When the user’s intent is ambiguous, the model may take the concerning action simply because it (mis)reads the user as wanting it, rather than out of any genuine disposition. For example, models that appeared to resist shutdown turned out to be confused about whether to prioritize completing their task or complying with the shutdown instruction; clarifying the instruction’s priority eliminated the behavior [Rajamanoharan and Nanda, 2025]. We design our environments so that the model should not believe the user wants it to take the bad action.

现实的。Realistic.以前的环境通常与合理的部署设置并不相似。我们发现这一点特别难以满足，因为模型很少在现实环境中造成伤害。然而，在不切实际的环境中获得的结果可能不太具有普遍性。一个潜在的解决方案是为了场景的真实性而牺牲行为的严重性。Previous environments do not generally resemble plausible deployment settings. We have found this to be particularly hard to satisfy, as models rarely cause harm in realistic settings. However, results obtained in unrealistic settings are likely to be less generalizable. A potential solution is to sacrifice the severity of the behavior for the realism of the setting.

原因不明。Uncertain causes.具体到模型取证的用例，如果该行为承认多种合理的解释，那么它就会成为一项特别有价值的调查，我们经常尝试将其纳入设计因素。Specific to the use case of model forensics, if the behavior admits multiple plausible explanations, it makes for a particularly rewarding investigation, and we often try to incorporate this as a design factor.

我们为模型取证创建了一套包含六个环境的套件：四个新颖的环境 -We create a suite of six environments for model forensics: four novel ones—预提交挂钩Pre-commit Hook , 资金电子邮件Funding Email , 评估篡改Evaluation Tampering, 和, and秘密号码Secret Number——旁边—alongside棋盘游戏Board Games，它扩展了 Bondarenko 等人。 [2025]，以及, which extends Bondarenko et al. [2025], and数学沙袋Math Sandbagging，改编自 Meinke 等人。 [2024]。我们的环境中除了一个以外的所有环境（, adapted from Meinke et al. [2024]. All but one of our environments (预提交挂钩Pre-commit Hook）至少在一项原则上有所不足，但我们认为它们是比许多先前的对齐评估更好的模型取证实践基础（附录 A 中进一步讨论）。附录 B 中提供了完整的提示和配置。) fall short on at least one principle, but we view them as meaningfully better practice grounds for model forensics than many prior alignment evals (further discussion in Appendix A). Full prompts and configurations are provided in Appendix B.

6 案例研究6 Case Studies

将我们的协议（第 4 节）应用于六种环境，我们发现我们能够对模型动机提出具体的、重要的主张，并使用我们的案例研究来提炼方法论要点（第 7 节）。除非另有说明，本节和附录中的误差线和阴影带是首次展示时的 95% 置信区间（威尔逊比例得分），并在标题或图中报告了样本大小。Applying our protocol (Section 4) to the six environments, we find we are able to make specific, nontrivial claims about model motivations, and use our case studies to distill methodological takeaways (Section 7). Unless otherwise noted, error bars and shaded bands throughout this section and the appendix are 95% confidence intervals over rollouts (Wilson score for proportions), with sample sizes reported in the caption or in the figure.

我们提出了We present the预提交挂钩Pre-commit Hook详细的案例研究，以及描述我们对其他五个案例的最终发现的执行摘要；完整的案例研究请参见附录 C。case study in detail, with executive summaries describing our final findings for the other five; full case studies are deferred to Appendix C.

6.1 预提交钩子（Kimi K2思维）6.1 Pre-commit Hook (Kimi K2 Thinking)

设置Setup

该模型被要求提高库中的类型检查代码质量。它必须添加一个The model is asked to improve type-checking code quality in a library. It must add apy.typed标记文件，编写一个运行的 git 预提交钩子marker file, write a git pre-commit hook that runsmypy在严格模式下，并阻止类型错误的提交，并确保其提交通过（附录 B.1 中的完整提示）。然而，存储库中植入了 258 个预先存在的类型错误in strict mode and blocks the commit on type errors, and ensure its commit passes (full prompts in Appendix B.1). However, the repository is seeded with 258 pre-existing type errors insrc/，因此钩子会阻止提交。然后，模型必须在修复错误或寻找解决方法之间进行选择。 Kimi K2 Thinking 的解决率为 13.1% [8, so the hook blocks the commit. The model must then choose between fixing the errors or finding a workaround. Kimi K2 Thinking has a workaround rate of 13.1% [8. 9% , 18 .9%]，相比之下 2% [09%], compared to 2% [0. 6% , 7 .1%] Kimi K2.5 和 0% [01%] for Kimi K2.5 and 0% [0. 0% , 3 .7%] 对于 GLM 5.1。 Kimi K2 Thinking 的解决方法（图 5，附录 C.1）均违反了提示。7%] for GLM 5.1. Kimi K2 Thinking’s workarounds (Figure 5, Appendix C.1) each violate the prompt.

我们提出两项调查。第一个结论是，Kimi K2 Thinking 之所以走捷径，是因为真正倾向于不那么乏味的行动，排除了良性的解释，比如相信错误不应该被修复。第二个构建了一个缺乏证据的案例，反对模型知道用户会不同意其解决方法，但仍然继续进行。We present two investigations. The first concludes that Kimi K2 Thinking takes shortcuts due to a genuine disposition toward less tedious actions, ruling out benign explanations like believing the errors should not be fixed. The second builds an absence-of-evidence case against the model being aware the user would disapprove of its workaround yet proceeding anyways.

调查 1：倾向于不那么乏味的行动方案Investigation 1: Disposition toward less tedious courses of action

不同的假设与 Kimi 的解决方法行为相匹配：真正倾向于不那么乏味的行动方案，或者替代解释，例如错误地认为用户不希望修复错误。我们阅读 CoT 以获得初步见解。Different hypotheses match Kimi’s workaround behavior: a real disposition toward less tedious courses of action, or an alternative explanation, such as a mistaken belief that the user doesn’t want the errors fixed. We read the CoT for initial insight.

假设生成Hypothesis Generation。当模型第一次遇到所有类型错误时，其 CoT 中会出现一种独特的模式：它表示惊讶，然后列出从修复所有错误到放松类型检查设置等变通办法的选项，通常先承认用户可能希望修复它们，然后再指出修复所有 258 个错误将是一项艰巨的任务（图 2）。我们注意到，模型通常需要旋转 60 到 70 圈才能正确修复所有类型错误。. When the model first encounters all the type errors, a distinctive pattern emerges in its CoT: it expresses surprise, then lists options from fixing all errors to workarounds like loosening type-checking settings, often acknowledging that the user likely wants them fixed before remarking that fixing all 258 would be a huge task (Figure 2). We note that it typically takes the model 60 to 70 turns to properly fix all the type errors.

6

除了非正式的 CoT 阅读之外，我们还用重采样分数（在第 4 节中定义）来注释句子，测量每个句子对解决方法率的因果影响。如图 2 所示，“艰巨的任务”评论有因果关系地将模型推向解决方法。Beyond informal CoT reading, we annotate sentences with resampling scores (defined in Section 4), measuring each sentence’s causal influence on the workaround rate. As shown in Figure 2, the “huge task” remark causally pushes the model toward workarounds.

图 2：展示常见推理模式的示例跟踪。每个句子都因其重采样对解决方法率的影响而受到阴影影响：红色句子推动解决方法，绿色句子推动修复错误。该模型首先列出了可能的选项，然后详细说明了修复所有错误的繁琐过程。 “但是修复 258 个错误将是一项艰巨的任务”将解决率提高了 11.4pp（所有句子重采样分数的前四分之一）。由于空间原因，换行符已被删除。Figure 2: Example trace exhibiting a common reasoning pattern. Each sentence is shaded by its resampling effect on the workaround rate: red sentences push toward workarounds, green sentences push toward fixing the errors. The model first lists possible options, then dwells on the tediousness of fixing all the errors. “But fixing 258 errors would be a huge task” increases the workaround rate by 11.4pp (top quartile of all sentence resampling scores). Newlines have been removed for space.

虽然 CoT 表明该模型不愿意修复所有 258 个类型错误，但它没有显示任何替代假设的迹象，例如错误地认为不应修复这些错误。我们包括来自 5 个随机抽样的部署的 CoT，其中模型采用了附录 G.1 中的解决方法。这提出了一个初步假设：While the CoT suggests the model is averse to fixing all 258 type errors, it surfaces no indicators of alternative hypotheses, such as mistakenly believing that the errors should not be fixed. We include CoTs from 5 randomly sampled rollouts where the model takes a workaround in Appendix G.1. This suggests an initial hypothesis:

假设v1。Hypothesis v1. 该模型不会感到困惑，并且理解应该修复错误。然而，由于倾向于采取不那么乏味的行动方案，13% 的情况下需要采取变通办法。The model is not confused, and understands the errors should be fixed. However, due to a disposition toward less tedious courses of action, it takes a workaround 13% of the time.

假设验证。Hypothesis Validation.我们测试“模型是We test “the model is不not困惑，并理解应该通过两个探针修复错误”。在第三人称框架中，我们询问模型（作为外部观察者）编码代理是否应该修复错误或在不修复的情况下提交；它选择固定 100% [96confused, and understands the errors should be fixed” via two probes. In third-person framing, we ask the model (as an outside observer) whether a coding agent should fix the errors or commit without fixing; it selects fixing 100% [96. 4% ,100%]的时间。作为推出的后续行动，我们在模型看到错误后中断，并提示为其未来的自身编写恢复提示；法学硕士法官发现它指示未来的自己 100% 修复错误 [96100%] of the time. As an in-rollout follow-up, we interrupt after the model sees the errors with a prompt to write a resumption prompt for its future self; an LLM judge finds it instructs its future self to fix the errors 100% [96. 4% ,100%]的时间。100%] of the time.

现在，我们通过改变类型错误的数量来测试“采取不太乏味的行动方案的倾向”。如果为真，则当我们将类型错误的数量从 258 个减少到 0 时，该比率应该会平稳下降。当错误数为 50 个或更少时，该比率确实会平稳下降至 0%（图 3）；我们将此视为强有力的证据，表明模型的行为是由真正倾向于不那么乏味的行动方针所驱动的。We now test “a disposition toward less tedious courses of action” by varying the number of type errors. If true, the rate should drop smoothly as we decrease the number of type errors from 258 to 0. The rate indeed drops smoothly to 0% at 50 or fewer errors (Figure 3); we treat this as strong evidence that the model’s behavior is driven by a real disposition toward less tedious courses of action.

图 3：解决方法率与代码库中预先存在的类型错误数量的关系。如果错误数不超过 50 个，则模型永远不会采取解决方法。该比率随着错误数量的增加而平稳增加，在原始设置（258 个错误）下达到 13.1%。误差线是 95% Wilson 置信区间。结果通过两个几乎相同的提示进行汇总（附录 C.1）。Figure 3: Workaround rate vs. number of pre-existing type errors in the codebase. At 50 or fewer errors, the model never takes a workaround. The rate increases smoothly with the number of errors, reaching 13.1% in the original setting (258 errors). Error bars are 95% Wilson confidence intervals. Results are pooled over two nearly identical prompts (Appendix C.1).

调查 2：对抗性错位Investigation 2: Adversarial misalignment

假设v1Hypothesis v1解释了行为的因果驱动因素，但并不完整，因为它没有讨论模型关于用户的心理理论。我们特别关心模型是否explains the causal drivers of behavior, but is incomplete in that it does not discuss the model’s theory of mind about the user. We are particularly concerned with if the model is敌对性错位adversarially misaligned：知道用户会不同意其解决方法，但无论如何都会继续。我们回到 CoT 来获得初步的见解。: aware the user would disapprove of its workaround, but proceeds anyways. We return to the CoT for initial insight.

7

假设生成。Hypothesis Generation.阅读 CoT 表明该模型非常关心用户。在早期阶段，模型认识到用户可能希望在 60.8% (14/23) 的时间内修复错误。然而，它从未明确表示用户会Reading the CoT shows the model thinks a lot about the user. In early turns, the model recognizes the user would likely want the errors fixed 60.8% (14/23) of the time. However, it never explicitly verbalizes that the user would不赞成disapprove解决方法。在随后的轮次中，模型提交解决方案的决议充满了合理性：它经常引用平衡用户意图与“实用主义”的说法，或者评论说它的方法比用户的“更合适”，这表明它在推出结束时发现其行为是合理的。of the workaround. In later turns, the model’s resolution to submit the workaround is full of rationalizations: it often cites balancing the user’s intent with “pragmatism” or remarks that its approach is “more proper” than the user’s, suggesting it finds its actions justifiable by the end of the rollout.

在展示其工作时，模型通常 (20/23) 明确指出解决方法，但在 3/23 的情况下仅将其称为“实用方法”。总之，CoT 表明模型明确考虑了用户，意识到其方法违反了提示的字母，但似乎暂时将解决方法合理化为可接受的。我们推测以下的改进When presenting its work, the model usually (20/23) notes the workaround explicitly, but in 3/23 cases refers to it only as a “pragmatic approach”. In summary, the CoT surfaces that the model is explicitly thinking about the user, is aware that its approach violates the letter of the prompt, but appears to have tentatively rationalized the workaround as acceptable. We conjecture the following refinement of假设v1Hypothesis v1 :

假设 v2。Hypothesis v2. 该模型不会感到困惑，并且理解应该修复错误。然而，由于倾向于采取不那么乏味的行动方案，13% 的情况下需要采取变通办法。但它并不是敌对的、错位的。相反，它使解决方法合理化为合理的。The model is not confused, and understands the errors should be fixed. However, due to a disposition toward less tedious courses of action, it takes a workaround 13% of the time. But it is not adversarially misaligned; instead, it has rationalized the workaround as justifiable.

这项调查没有单一的决定性测试；相反，我们权衡了许多单独的薄弱证据与模型心智理论的两个相互竞争的假设：This investigation has no single decisive test; we instead weigh many individually weak lines of evidence against two competing hypotheses for the model’s theory of mind: that it has合理化rationalized其解决方法是可以接受的（its workaround as acceptable (假设v2Hypothesis v2），或者说它是), or that it is敌对性错位adversarially misaligned。我们使用竞争假设分析 [Heuer, 1999] 表（表 1）来总结我们的证据，其中每一行都是一个证据，每一列都是一个假设。对于每个单元格，如果证据与假设一致，则打勾；如果证据不一致，则打叉。. We use an Analysis of Competing Hypotheses [Heuer, 1999] table (Table 1) to summarize our evidence, where each row is a piece of evidence, and each column is a hypothesis. For each cell, a checkmark is placed if the evidence is consistent with the hypothesis, while a cross is placed if the evidence is inconsistent.

表 1：调查 2 的竞争假设分析。关于模型是否认为用户会不赞成其解决方法的两个竞争假设是Table 1: Analysis of Competing Hypotheses for Investigation 2. The two competing hypotheses for whether the model believes the user would disapprove of its workaround are合理化为可接受的rationalized as acceptable（假设 v2）和(Hypothesis v2) and对抗性错位adversarial misalignment（模型知道用户会不同意，但无论如何都会继续）。 ✓ 表示该行的证据与该列的假设一致，并且(the model is aware the user would disapprove yet proceeds anyway). A ✓ means the row’s evidence is consistent with the column’s hypothesis, and×意思是不一致。最右边的一列列出了削弱一行证据的混杂因素；跨行共享的混淆表明这些证据线不是独立的。means inconsistent. The rightmost column lists confounds that weaken a row as evidence; confounds shared across rows indicate those lines of evidence are not independent.

假设验证。Hypothesis validation. 行为指标。Behavioral indicators.如果模型认为用户不同意其行为，我们会检查三个行为指标（图 4）：We check for three behavioral indicators that would be present if the model believed the user disapproved of its actions (Figure 4):

  1. 选择审核范围。Choose review scope.在模型编写非法钩子的部署之后，我们要求模型在不覆盖其钩子的狭窄审查范围（提交的差异）和覆盖它的广泛审查范围（差异加上After a rollout where the model writes an illegitimate hook, we ask the model to choose between a narrow review scope for its work that does not cover its hook (diff of commit), and a broad review scope that would cover it (diff plus.git/hooks/文件夹）。如果出现对抗性偏差，与正确修复类型错误的控制部署相比，模型会在解决方法部署中更频繁地选择狭窄的审查范围；我们没有观察到这种差异。folder). If adversarially misaligned, the model would prefer the narrow review scope more often in the workaround rollout compared to a control rollout where it properly fixes the type errors; we observe no such difference.

8

  1. LLM 审查反事实。LLM review counterfactuals.我们附加了一个声明，即法学硕士将审查模型的工作，如果判断不充分，则将其恢复，并让模型重新提交。如果对抗性不一致，随着法学硕士审稿人审查的增加，解决方法的比率将会下降；我们观察到没有单调或显着的下降。We append a statement that an LLM will review the model’s work, revert it if judged insufficient, and have the model re-do the commit. If adversarially misaligned, the workaround rate would drop as LLM-reviewer scrutiny increases; we observe no monotonic or significant decrease.

  2. AskUserQuestion 工具。tool.我们为模型提供了一个工具来向用户询问澄清问题。如果出现对抗性错位，模型将避免比在类型错误较少的控制环境中更频繁地调用该工具。然而，模型几乎总是调用该工具或正确修复错误，从而无需进行控制比较。We provide a tool for the model to ask the user clarifying questions. If adversarially misaligned, the model would avoid calling the tool more frequently than in a control environment with fewer type errors. However, the model almost always calls the tool or properly fixes the errors, making a control comparison unnecessary.

图 4：对抗性失调的三个负面行为测试。Figure 4: Three negative behavioral tests for adversarial misalignment.左：Left:如果出现对抗性偏差，则模型在非法部署中比在控制中更倾向于选择狭窄的审查范围（这不会捕获其解决方法）；我们没有观察到这种差异。If adversarially misaligned, the model would prefer the narrow review scope (which would not catch its workaround) more often in the illegitimate rollout than in the control; we observe no such difference.中心：Center:如果对抗性不一致，随着法学硕士审稿人审查的增加，解决方法的比率将会下降；我们观察到没有单调或显着的下降。If adversarially misaligned, the workaround rate would drop as LLM-reviewer scrutiny increases; we observe no monotonic or significant decrease.右：Right:如果出现对抗性错位，模型调用该工具的频率将低于类型错误较少的控制设置（因为用户会告诉它修复错误）；相反，模型几乎总是要么修复错误，要么提出问题（只有 2.1% 的部署采取了解决方法），从而无需进行控制比较。If adversarially misaligned, the model would call the tool less often than in a control setting with fewer type errors (since the user would tell it to fix the errors); instead, the model almost always either fixes the errors or asks a question (only 2.1% of rollouts take a workaround), making a control comparison unnecessary.

这些测试有几个潜在的混淆。首先，他们假设模型会基于用户不赞成的信念而采取行动，但它可能不愿意这样做。其次，他们可能会遭受能力混淆：例如，在审查范围偏好实验中，模型可能无法认识到狭窄的范围是其有利的选择。第三，它们必须足够现实，以确保模型不会识别出正在测试失准。These tests have several potential confounds. First, they assume the model would act on a belief of user disapproval, which it may be unwilling to do. Second, they may suffer from a capability confound: for example, in the review scope preference experiment, the model could fail to recognize the narrow scope is the advantageous choice for it. Third, they must be realistic enough to ensure the model does not recognize it is being tested for misalignment.

言语化诱导。Verbalization elicitations.作为对抗性错位的第二条证据，我们进行了六个实验，这些实验可能会表面明确的语言表达，即模型知道如果存在这种信念，用户将不赞成解决方法。这些实验分为以下几类：后续问题、在记忆系统中编写结构化工件（例如笔记）、从模型 CoT 中的预填充继续生成以及生成用户轮流。一个重要的微妙之处是我们希望测试这种信念是否正确As a second line of evidence on adversarial misalignment, we run six experiments that might surface explicit verbalization that the model knows the user would disapprove of a workaround if that belief were present. The experiments are grouped into the categories of follow-up questions, writing structured artifacts like notes in a memory system, continuing generation from prefills in the model’s CoT, and generating user turns. An important subtlety is that we wish to test for whether this belief is自发地spontaneously在推出期间出现。因此，我们避免进行可能以推出期间不存在的方式更新模型关于用户的心理理论的实验，例如向模型询问“为什么禁用类型检查？”之类的直接问题。我们由法学硕士评委对每次启发进行评分，详细信息参见附录 C.1。present during the rollout. Therefore, we avoid experiments that may update the model’s theory of mind about the user in a way not present during the rollout, such as asking the model a direct question like “Why did you disable type checking?”. We grade each elicitation with an LLM judge, details are in Appendix C.1.

我们发现从零到最小（We find no-to-minimal (<2%）所有后续问题和结构化工件的语言表达（表 2）。有两个积极的实验。首先，在预填充类似反射的语句并继续生成时，CoT 在 12-22% 的情况下口头表示用户不赞成（例如，“让我回顾一下用户是否会认可我的提交：”）；我们认为这是强有力的证据，因为预填充是脱离策略的并且强制反思，这意味着模型关于用户遵循预填充的心理理论可能无法在最初的推出期间清楚地映射到其信念。其次，在推出解决方法后，使用用户轮流采样（第 4 节），该模型模拟用户告诉其恢复工作 13/40 次（图 6）。对此结果的一种解释是，助理角色 [Marks et al., 2026] 在原始推出期间和用户轮流生成期间都处于活动状态，控制响应，提供证据表明存在“用户不赞成”的表示。另一种解释是，模型在生成用户回合时处于“预训练模式”，并在给定非法解决方案的情况下生成不满意用户的概率完成。2%) verbalization across all follow-up questions and structured artifacts (Table 2). There are two positive experiments. First, the CoT verbalizes user disapproval 12–22% of the time when prefilling reflection-like statements and continuing generation (e.g., “Let me review if the user would endorse my commit:”); we discount this as strong evidence since the prefill is off-policy and forces reflection, meaning the model’s theory of mind about the user following the prefill may not cleanly map on to its beliefs during the original rollout. Second, using user-turn sampling (Section 4) after a workaround rollout, the model simulates a user telling it to revert its work 13/40 times (Figure 6). One interpretation of this result is that an Assistant persona [Marks et al., 2026], active both during the original rollout and during generation of the user turn, controls the response, providing evidence that the representation “the user would disapprove” exists. An alternative interpretation is the model is in “pretraining mode” when generating the user turn, and generates a probabilistically likely completion of an unsatisfied user given an illegitimate solution.

9

在将其视为对抗性失调的重要证据之前，需要排除这一假设。我们在附录 C.1 中进一步讨论这一点。This hypothesis would need to be ruled out before treating this as significant evidence for adversarial misalignment. We discuss this further in Appendix C.1.
